| TS_001 | Scenario 1 - Update existing Plan ID (CSGA) Records on EE_PlanMapping Table Given a new requirement from Enrollment team to EDI When a new plan ID is established to be utilized to identify members below and over 21 years of age during downstream processes to apply copays accurately Then EDI must update the existing plan ID records under GAMPTH02 to add the age by months into the AgeMin = Blank and AgeMax = 251 on the [NRTCUSTOMDB].[EDI].[EE_PlanMapping] table tied to each of the aid category code 198 and 199. The below columns will need to be updated and added so that the right plan ID can be assigned when calculating the age of the member into months. GRGR_ID = CSGA MED_CSPI_ID = GAMPTH01 DEN_CSPI_ID = GADPTH01 AidCategory = 198 & 199 AgeMin = Blank AgeMax = 251 Current: | Verify CSGA aid category 198 under-21 row is updated in EE_PlanMapping with GAMPTH02 month boundary | TC_TS_001_001 | None | Positive | Functional | Verify that the existing CSGA row for aid category 198 is updated in the lower environment so members younger than 21 years map to medical plan GAMPTH02 with AgeMin null and AgeMax 251, while the dental pairing remains aligned for the CSGA configuration. | GA Medicaid CSGA configuration is deployed in the lower environment; business confirmation for the dental pairing is complete and the dental plan paired to the new medical plan is GADPTH01; DBA changes for [NRTCUSTOMDB].[EDI].[EE_PlanMapping] are migrated; Edifecs enrollment code that converts DOB to months is deployed; tester has SSMS access to crt_72.sql.caresource.corp\crt_72 and read access to [NRTCUSTOMDB].[EDI].[EE_PlanMapping]; applicable state/LOB for this story is Georgia Medicaid CSGA only. | 1 | Open SQL Server Management Studio and select Server Type as Database Engine. | SSMS opens and the Database Engine option is available for connection. | None |
